function canonical(s){return (s||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'')}
const LOOKUP={};DATA.forEach(d=>{d.rendaPC=d.renda&&d.resp&&d.morDom?Math.round(d.renda*d.resp/d.morDom*100)/100:null;LOOKUP[canonical(d.b)]=d});
function findData(name){return LOOKUP[canonical(name)]||null}

function fmt(v,kind){
  if(v==null||v===undefined||isNaN(v)) return '—';
  if(kind==='int') return Math.round(v).toLocaleString('pt-BR');
  if(kind==='pct') return (v*100).toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1})+'%';
  if(kind==='money') return 'R$ '+v.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:0,maximumFractionDigits:0});
  if(kind==='float1') return v.toLocaleString('pt-BR',{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1});
  return String(v);
}
function destroyChart(id){if(CHARTS[id]){CHARTS[id].destroy();delete CHARTS[id]}}
function baseOpts(h){return{indexAxis:h?'y':'x',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{backgroundColor:'#14324a',titleFont:{family:'Inter',size:12,weight:'700'},bodyFont:{family:'Inter',size:12},cornerRadius:10,padding:10}},scales:{x:{grid:{color:'#edf2f6'},ticks:{font:{family:'Inter',size:10},color:'#7a8d9f'}},y:{grid:{color:'#edf2f6'},ticks:{font:{family:'Inter',size:10},color:'#7a8d9f'}}}}}
function makeBar(id,labels,datasets,extra){destroyChart(id);const canvas=document.getElementById(id);const barH=Math.max(labels.length*28,300);canvas.style.height=barH+'px';canvas.parentElement.style.height='auto';const o=baseOpts(true);o.scales=o.scales||{};o.scales.y=o.scales.y||{};o.scales.y.ticks={...(o.scales.y.ticks||{}),autoSkip:false,maxTicksLimit:labels.length};if(extra?.plugins)o.plugins={...o.plugins,...extra.plugins};CHARTS[id]=new Chart(canvas,{type:'bar',data:{labels,datasets},options:o})}

const CHOROPLETH_COLORS=['#F3E8FF','#DDD6FE','#C084FC','#A855F7','#7C3AED','#5B21B6'];
const VULN_COLORS=['#F3E8FF','#DDD6FE','#C084FC','#A855F7','#7C3AED','#4C1D95'];
function calcBreaks(values,steps){
  const sorted=[...values].filter(v=>v!=null&&!isNaN(v)).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  if(!sorted.length) return Array(steps+1).fill(0);
  const breaks=[sorted[0]];
  for(let i=1;i<=steps;i++)breaks.push(sorted[Math.min(Math.floor(i*sorted.length/steps),sorted.length-1)]);
  return breaks;
}
function getColor(v,breaks,colors){
  if(v==null||isNaN(v)) return '#e8f0e9';
  for(let i=colors.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(v>=breaks[i]) return colors[i];
  return colors[0];
}

const INDICATORS={
  pop:{label:'População total (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'int',higher:'alto'},
  dens:{label:'Densidade demográfica (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'float1',higher:'alto'},
  raca:{label:'Pop. não-branca (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'alto'},
  mulh:{label:'Chefia feminina (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'alto'},
  cad:{label:'Incidência CadÚnico (2025)',fmt:'pct',higher:'alto'},
  epob:{label:'Extrema pobreza (MDS, 2025)',fmt:'pct',higher:'alto'},
  anal:{label:'Analfabetismo 15+ (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'alto'},
  esg:{label:'Rede de esgoto (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'bom'},
  agua:{label:'Rede de água (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'bom'},
  lixo:{label:'Coleta de lixo (IBGE, 2022)',fmt:'pct',higher:'bom'},
  emp:{label:'Empresas formais (dez/2024)',fmt:'int',higher:'alto'},
  osc:{label:'OSCs ativas (IPEA, 2024)',fmt:'int',higher:'alto'},
  renda:{label:'Rendimento médio do responsável (IBGE Censo, 2022)',fmt:'money',higher:'bom'},
  rendaPC:{label:'Rendimento do responsável per capita (IBGE Censo, 2022)',fmt:'money',higher:'bom'},
};

function createMap(containerId){
  if(MAPS[containerId]){MAPS[containerId].map.remove();delete MAPS[containerId]}
  const map=L.map(containerId,{
    zoomControl:false,
    attributionControl:true,
    preferCanvas:true,
    renderer:L.canvas({padding:0.5}),
    zoomSnap:0.25
  });
  map.attributionControl.setPrefix(false);
  map.createPane('basemap');
  map.getPane('basemap').style.zIndex='200';
  map.getPane('basemap').style.opacity='0.76';
  map.getPane('basemap').style.filter='grayscale(1) contrast(1.02) brightness(1.03)';
  map.createPane('fills');
  map.getPane('fills').style.zIndex='320';
  map.createPane('outlines');
  map.getPane('outlines').style.zIndex='360';
  map.createPane('labels');
  map.getPane('labels').style.zIndex='450';
  map.getPane('labels').style.pointerEvents='none';
  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_nolabels/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{
    subdomains:'abcd',maxZoom:20,pane:'basemap',
    attribution:'&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors &copy; CARTO'
  }).addTo(map);
  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_only_labels/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{
    subdomains:'abcd',maxZoom:20,pane:'labels',
    attribution:'&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors &copy; CARTO'
  }).addTo(map);
  L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(map);
  MAPS[containerId]={map,layer:null};
  return map;
}

function paintMap(containerId,indicatorKey,colorSet){
  const m=MAPS[containerId];if(!m) return;
  const colors=colorSet||CHOROPLETH_COLORS;
  const ind=INDICATORS[indicatorKey];
  const values=DATA.map(d=>d[indicatorKey]).filter(v=>v!=null&&!isNaN(v));
  const breaks=calcBreaks(values,colors.length);
  
  if(m.layer) m.map.removeLayer(m.layer);
  m.layer=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
    pane:'fills',
    renderer:m.map.options.renderer,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:feature=>{
      const d=findData(feature.properties.nome);
      const v=d?d[indicatorKey]:null;
      return{fillColor:getColor(v,breaks,colors),weight:0,stroke:false,fillOpacity:.78};
    },
    onEachFeature:(feature,layer)=>{
      const d=findData(feature.properties.nome);
      if(!d) return;
      const popupHTML=`<div class="popup-card"><h4>${d.b}</h4><div class="popup-grid">
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.pop,'int')}</strong><span>População</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.epob,'pct')}</strong><span>Ext. pobreza</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.rendaPC,'money')}</strong><span>Rend. resp. per capita</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.renda,'money')}</strong><span>Renda responsável</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.esg,'pct')}</strong><span>Esgoto</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.cad,'pct')}</strong><span>CadÚnico</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.emp,'int')}</strong><span>Empresas</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.osc,'int')}</strong><span>OSCs</span></div>
      </div></div>`;
      layer.bindPopup(popupHTML,{maxWidth:340});
      layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({fillOpacity:.95})});
      layer.on('mouseout',function(){m.layer.resetStyle(this)});
    }
  }).addTo(m.map);
  // boundary outlines layer
  if(m.outlines) m.map.removeLayer(m.outlines);
  m.outlines=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
    pane:'outlines',
    renderer:m.map.options.renderer,
    interactive:false,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:()=>({color:'#c7cfd8',weight:1.05,opacity:1,fill:false,lineCap:'round',lineJoin:'round'})
  }).addTo(m.map);
  m.map.fitBounds(m.layer.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});

  const prefix=containerId.replace('map-','');
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('legend-title-'+prefix);
  const minEl=document.getElementById('legend-min-'+prefix);
  const maxEl=document.getElementById('legend-max-'+prefix);
  if(titleEl) titleEl.textContent=ind?.label||indicatorKey;
  if(minEl) minEl.textContent=fmt(breaks[0],ind?.fmt);
  if(maxEl) maxEl.textContent=fmt(breaks[breaks.length-1],ind?.fmt);
}

function paintMapFiltered(containerId,indicatorKey,colorSet,regionFilter,bairroFilter){
  const m=MAPS[containerId];if(!m) return;
  const colors=colorSet||CHOROPLETH_COLORS;
  const ind=INDICATORS[indicatorKey];
  const values=DATA.map(d=>d[indicatorKey]).filter(v=>v!=null&&!isNaN(v));
  const breaks=calcBreaks(values,colors.length);

  if(m.layer) m.map.removeLayer(m.layer);
  m.layer=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
    pane:'fills',
    renderer:m.map.options.renderer,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:feature=>{
      const d=findData(feature.properties.nome);
      const v=d?d[indicatorKey]:null;
      let visible=true;
      if(d && regionFilter && (REGION_MAP[d.b]||'')!==regionFilter) visible=false;
      if(d && bairroFilter && d.b!==bairroFilter) visible=false;
      return{
        fillColor: visible ? getColor(v,breaks,colors) : '#f0f0f0',
        weight:0,stroke:false,
        fillOpacity: visible ? 0.78 : 0.25
      };
    },
    onEachFeature:(feature,layer)=>{
      const d=findData(feature.properties.nome);
      if(!d) return;
      const popupHTML=`<div class="popup-card"><h4>${d.b}</h4><div class="popup-grid">
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.pop,'int')}</strong><span>População</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.epob,'pct')}</strong><span>Ext. pobreza</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.rendaPC,'money')}</strong><span>Rend. resp. per capita</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.renda,'money')}</strong><span>Renda responsável</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.esg,'pct')}</strong><span>Esgoto</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.cad,'pct')}</strong><span>CadÚnico</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.emp,'int')}</strong><span>Empresas</span></div>
        <div class="popup-stat"><strong>${fmt(d.osc,'int')}</strong><span>OSCs</span></div>
      </div></div>`;
      layer.bindPopup(popupHTML,{maxWidth:340});
      layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({fillOpacity:.95})});
      layer.on('mouseout',function(){m.layer.resetStyle(this)});
    }
  }).addTo(m.map);

  if(m.outlines) m.map.removeLayer(m.outlines);
  m.outlines=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
    pane:'outlines',
    renderer:m.map.options.renderer,
    interactive:false,
    smoothFactor:0,
    style:()=>({color:'#c7cfd8',weight:1.05,opacity:1,fill:false,lineCap:'round',lineJoin:'round'})
  }).addTo(m.map);

  if(bairroFilter){
    m.layer.eachLayer(l=>{
      const d=findData(l.feature?.properties?.nome);
      if(d && d.b===bairroFilter) m.map.fitBounds(l.getBounds(),{padding:[40,40]});
    });
  } else {
    m.map.fitBounds(m.layer.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});
  }

  const prefix=containerId.replace('map-','');
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('legend-title-'+prefix);
  const minEl=document.getElementById('legend-min-'+prefix);
  const maxEl=document.getElementById('legend-max-'+prefix);
  if(titleEl) titleEl.textContent=ind?.label||indicatorKey;
  if(minEl) minEl.textContent=fmt(breaks[0],ind?.fmt);
  if(maxEl) maxEl.textContent=fmt(breaks[breaks.length-1],ind?.fmt);
}

function populateSelect(id,indicators){
  const sel=document.getElementById(id);
  sel.innerHTML=indicators.map(k=>`<option value="${k}">${INDICATORS[k].label}</option>`).join('');
  return sel;
}


/* ===== MAP ANALYSIS HELPERS ===== */
function mean(arr,key){const v=arr.filter(d=>d[key]!=null&&!isNaN(d[key]));return v.length?v.reduce((s,d)=>s+d[key],0)/v.length:null}
function median(arr,key){const v=arr.map(d=>d[key]).filter(x=>x!=null&&!isNaN(x)).sort((a,b)=>a-b);if(!v.length)return null;const m=Math.floor(v.length/2);return v.length%2?v[m]:(v[m-1]+v[m])/2}
function renderAnalysis(section,indicatorKey){
  const ind=INDICATORS[indicatorKey];if(!ind)return;
  const titleEl=document.getElementById('map-summary-title-'+section);
  const textEl=document.getElementById('map-summary-'+section);
  const insightsEl=document.getElementById('map-insights-'+section);
  if(!titleEl||!textEl||!insightsEl)return;
  const valid=DATA.filter(d=>d[indicatorKey]!=null&&!isNaN(d[indicatorKey]));
  if(!valid.length){titleEl.textContent=ind.label;textEl.textContent='Dados insuficientes para este indicador.';insightsEl.innerHTML='';return;}
  const sorted=[...valid].sort((a,b)=>b[indicatorKey]-a[indicatorKey]);
  const top=sorted[0];const bottom=sorted[sorted.length-1];
  const avg=mean(valid,indicatorKey);const med=median(valid,indicatorKey);
  const amplitude=top[indicatorKey]-bottom[indicatorKey];
  titleEl.textContent=ind.label+' — Maracanaú';
  const highBad=['cad','epob','anal'].includes(indicatorKey);
  const lowBad=['esg','agua','lixo'].includes(indicatorKey);
  const contextNote=highBad
    ?'Os bairros com maior índice concentram situações de vulnerabilidade que demandam atenção prioritária.'
    :lowBad
    ?'Bairros com menor cobertura indicam déficits de infraestrutura que afetam a qualidade de vida.'
    :'O mapa revela contrastes internos relevantes para o planejamento territorial de Maracanaú.';
  textEl.innerHTML='<strong>'+top.b+'</strong> registra o maior valor em '+ind.label.toLowerCase()+' (<strong>'+fmt(top[indicatorKey],ind.fmt)+'</strong>), enquanto <strong>'+bottom.b+'</strong> apresenta o menor (<strong>'+fmt(bottom[indicatorKey],ind.fmt)+'</strong>). A amplitude interna é <strong>'+fmt(amplitude,ind.fmt)+'</strong> e a média municipal é <strong>'+fmt(avg,ind.fmt)+'</strong>. '+contextNote;
  const noteEl=document.getElementById('note-'+section);
  if(noteEl) noteEl.innerHTML='<strong>'+ind.label+'.</strong> Maior valor: <strong>'+top.b+'</strong> ('+fmt(top[indicatorKey],ind.fmt)+') · Menor valor: <strong>'+bottom.b+'</strong> ('+fmt(bottom[indicatorKey],ind.fmt)+') · Média: <strong>'+fmt(avg,ind.fmt)+'</strong>.';
  insightsEl.innerHTML=[
    {label:'Maior valor',value:fmt(top[indicatorKey],ind.fmt),sub:top.b},
    {label:'Menor valor',value:fmt(bottom[indicatorKey],ind.fmt),sub:bottom.b},
    {label:'Média municipal',value:fmt(avg,ind.fmt),sub:'Base: '+valid.length+' bairros'},
    {label:'Mediana',value:fmt(med,ind.fmt),sub:'Valor central da distribuição'}
  ].map(c=>'<div class="analysis-stat"><div class="analysis-stat-label">'+c.label+'</div><div class="analysis-stat-value">'+c.value+'</div><div class="analysis-stat-sub">'+c.sub+'</div></div>').join('');
}

/* ===== INIT FUNCTIONS ===== */
function initOverview(){
  const totalPop=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.pop||0),0);
  const totalEmp=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.emp||0),0);
  const totalRendaPC=totalPop?DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.rendaPC||0)*(d.pop||0),0)/totalPop:0;
  const totalRenda=totalPop?DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.renda||0)*(d.pop||0),0)/totalPop:0;
  document.getElementById('overview-kpis').innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">População total</div><div class="value">${fmt(totalPop,'int')}</div><div class="sub">${DATA.length} bairros · Censo 2022</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Empresas ativas</div><div class="value">${fmt(totalEmp,'int')}</div><div class="sub">Receita Federal · dez/2024</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Rendimento resp. per capita</div><div class="value">${fmt(totalRendaPC,'money')}</div><div class="sub">IBGE Censo, 2022</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Renda do responsável</div><div class="value">${fmt(totalRenda,'money')}</div><div class="sub">IBGE Censo, 2022</div></div>`;
  
  const sel=populateSelect('visao-indicador',['epob','esg','cad','pop','rendaPC','renda','osc','emp','anal']);
  createMap('map-visao');

  // Populate region filter
  const regSelV=document.getElementById('visao-regiao');
  regSelV.innerHTML='<option value="">Todas as regiões</option>'+REGION_ORDER.map(r=>`<option value="${r}">${r}</option>`).join('');
  // Populate bairro filter
  const bairroSelV=document.getElementById('visao-bairro');
  const allBairrosVisao=DATA.map(d=>d.b).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  bairroSelV.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>'+allBairrosVisao.map(b=>`<option value="${b}">${b}</option>`).join('');

  function visaoColorSet(k){ return ['epob','cad','anal'].includes(k)?VULN_COLORS:CHOROPLETH_COLORS; }
  function updateVisaoFiltered(){
    const k=sel.value;
    paintMapFiltered('map-visao',k,visaoColorSet(k),regSelV.value,bairroSelV.value);
    renderAnalysis('visao',k);
    updateVisaoChart(k);
  }
  paintMapFiltered('map-visao','epob',VULN_COLORS,'','');
  renderAnalysis('visao','epob');
  sel.addEventListener('change',updateVisaoFiltered);
  regSelV.addEventListener('change',()=>{
    const reg=regSelV.value;
    const filteredBairros=reg?DATA.filter(d=>(REGION_MAP[d.b]||'')===reg).map(d=>d.b).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR')):allBairrosVisao;
    bairroSelV.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>'+filteredBairros.map(b=>`<option value="${b}">${b}</option>`).join('');
    updateVisaoFiltered();
  });
  bairroSelV.addEventListener('change',updateVisaoFiltered);

  const sorted=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(b.epob||0)-(a.epob||0));
  makeBar('chart-visao-epob',sorted.map(d=>d.b),[{data:sorted.map(d=>((d.epob||0)*100).toFixed(1)),backgroundColor:sorted.map(d=>(d.epob||0)>.4?UI.danger:(d.epob||0)>.25?UI.gold:UI.blue),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.parsed.x+'%'}}}});
  const sortedRenda=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(b.rendaPC||0)-(a.rendaPC||0));
  makeBar('chart-visao-rendapc',sortedRenda.map(d=>d.b),[{data:sortedRenda.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC||0)),backgroundColor:sortedRenda.map(d=>(d.rendaPC||0)>2000?UI.blue:(d.rendaPC||0)>1000?UI.gold:UI.danger),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}});
}

function updateVisaoChart(k){
  // Only epob and rendapc are shown in the fixed charts at bottom
  // Other indicators update the map and analysis, but not the fixed charts
  if(k==='epob'){
    const ind=INDICATORS[k];
    const data=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(b.epob||0)-(a.epob||0));
    const chart=CHARTS['chart-visao-epob'];
    if(chart){
      chart.data.labels=data.map(d=>d.b);
      chart.data.datasets[0].data=data.map(d=>((d.epob||0)*100).toFixed(1));
      chart.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor=data.map(d=>(d.epob||0)>.4?UI.danger:(d.epob||0)>.25?UI.gold:UI.blue);
      chart.update('none');
    }
  } else if(k==='rendapc' || k==='rendaPC'){
    const data=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(b.rendaPC||0)-(a.rendaPC||0));
    const chart=CHARTS['chart-visao-rendapc'];
    if(chart){
      chart.data.labels=data.map(d=>d.b);
      chart.data.datasets[0].data=data.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC||0));
      chart.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor=data.map(d=>(d.rendaPC||0)>2000?UI.blue:(d.rendaPC||0)>1000?UI.gold:UI.danger);
      chart.update('none');
    }
  }
}

  // Rendimento do responsável per capita (Censo 2022)
  const byRendaPC=[...DATA].filter(d=>d.rendaPC!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.rendaPC-b.rendaPC);
  makeBar('chart-vuln-rendapc',byRendaPC.map(d=>d.b),[{data:byRendaPC.map(d=>Math.round(d.rendaPC)),backgroundColor:byRendaPC.map(d=>d.rendaPC<450?UI.danger:d.rendaPC<600?UI.gold:UI.teal),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}});
  // Rendimento médio do responsável (Censo 2022)
  const byRenda=[...DATA].sort((a,b)=>(a.renda||0)-(b.renda||0));
  makeBar('chart-vuln-renda',byRenda.map(d=>d.b),[{data:byRenda.map(d=>Math.round(d.renda||0)),backgroundColor:byRenda.map(d=>(d.renda||0)<1300?UI.danger:(d.renda||0)<1600?UI.gold:UI.teal),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>'R$ '+Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}}}});
  // Moradores por domicílio (Censo 2022) — média = morDom/resp
  const byMorDom=[...DATA].filter(d=>d.resp>0).sort((a,b)=>(b.morDom/b.resp)-(a.morDom/a.resp));
  makeBar('chart-vuln-mordom',byMorDom.map(d=>d.b),[{data:byMorDom.map(d=>(d.morDom/d.resp).toFixed(2)),backgroundColor:byMorDom.map(d=>(d.morDom/d.resp)>2.3?UI.danger:(d.morDom/d.resp)>2.0?UI.gold:UI.blue),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.parsed.x+' moradores/domicílio'}}}});
  // Responsáveis por domicílio (Censo 2022)
  const byResp=[...DATA].filter(d=>d.resp>0).sort((a,b)=>b.resp-a.resp);
  makeBar('chart-vuln-resp',byResp.map(d=>d.b),[{data:byResp.map(d=>d.resp),backgroundColor:UI.blue,borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>Number(ctx.parsed.x).toLocaleString('pt-BR')+' responsáveis'}}}});
}
