function fmtCap(v){
  if(v>=1e12) return 'R$ '+(v/1e12).toFixed(1)+' tri';
  if(v>=1e9) return 'R$ '+(v/1e9).toFixed(1)+' bi';
  if(v>=1e6) return 'R$ '+(v/1e6).toFixed(1)+' M';
  if(v>=1e3) return 'R$ '+(v/1e3).toFixed(0)+' mil';
  return 'R$ '+v.toFixed(0);
}
function fmtDateBR(s){
  if(!s||typeof s!=='string'||s.length<10) return '';
  const [y,m,d]=s.slice(0,10).split('-');
  if(!y||!m||!d) return '';
  return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
}
function ageYears(s){
  if(!s||typeof s!=='string'||s.length<10) return null;
  const dt=new Date(s.slice(0,10));
  if(isNaN(dt)) return null;
  const now=new Date();
  let y=now.getFullYear()-dt.getFullYear();
  if(now.getMonth()<dt.getMonth()||(now.getMonth()===dt.getMonth()&&now.getDate()<dt.getDate())) y--;
  return y>=0?y:null;
}
function fmtCnae(c){
  if(!c) return '';
  const s=String(c).padStart(7,'0');
  return `${s.slice(0,4)}-${s[4]}/${s.slice(5)}`;
}
function mgeRadius(cap){
  if(cap<=0) return 4;
  return Math.min(Math.max(Math.log10(Math.max(cap,1))*2.8,4),40);
}
function mgeColor(cap){
  if(cap>=1e9) return '#c0392b';
  if(cap>=1e8) return '#e74c3c';
  if(cap>=1e7) return '#e67e22';
  if(cap>=1e6) return '#f39c12';
  if(cap>=1e5) return '#7C3AED';
  return '#C084FC';
}

function initEcon(){
  // --- Economia: KPIs resumo geral ---
  const totalEmpAll=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.emp||0),0);
  const totalMEI=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.mei||0),0);
  const totalCoops=DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.coops||0),0);
  const avgMeiAll=DATA.filter(d=>d.meiShare!=null).reduce((s,d,_,a)=>s+d.meiShare/a.length,0);
  const econResumo=document.getElementById('kpi-econ-resumo');
  if(econResumo) econResumo.innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Total de empresas</div><div class="value">${totalEmpAll.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Todos os portes · Receita Federal, 2024</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">MEIs</div><div class="value">${totalMEI.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">${(avgMeiAll*100).toFixed(0)}% do total em média</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Micro e pequenas</div><div class="value">${DATA.reduce((s,d)=>s+(+d.mpe||0),0).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Porte MPE</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Cooperativas</div><div class="value">${totalCoops.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Cooperativas ativas</div></div>`;

  // --- Original charts ---
  const withEmp=DATA.filter(d=>d.emp>0).sort((a,b)=>b.emp-a.emp);
  makeBar('chart-econ-emp',withEmp.map(d=>d.b),[{data:withEmp.map(d=>d.emp),backgroundColor:UI.blue,borderRadius:5}]);
  const withMei=DATA.filter(d=>d.meiShare!=null).sort((a,b)=>b.meiShare-a.meiShare);
  makeBar('chart-econ-mei',withMei.map(d=>d.b),[{data:withMei.map(d=>(d.meiShare*100).toFixed(1)),backgroundColor:withMei.map(d=>d.meiShare>.8?UI.gold:d.meiShare>.6?UI.blue:UI.teal),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.parsed.x+'%'}}}});

  // --- MGE KPIs ---
  const totalMGE=MGE_BUBBLES.length;
  const totalCap=MGE_BAIRRO.reduce((s,b)=>s+b.cap,0);
  const topBairro=MGE_BAIRRO[0];
  const topSetor=MGE_SETOR[0];
  const avgMeiPct=DATA.filter(d=>d.meiShare!=null).reduce((s,d,_,a)=>s+d.meiShare/a.length,0);
  const matrizes=MGE_BUBBLES.filter(r=>r[6]==='M').length;
  document.getElementById('kpi-mge').innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Empresas M/G</div><div class="value">${totalMGE.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">Receita Federal · dez/2024</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Capital total</div><div class="value">${fmtCap(totalCap)}</div><div class="sub">Capital social acumulado</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Maior concentração</div><div class="value">${topBairro.b}</div><div class="sub">${topBairro.n} empresas · ${fmtCap(topBairro.cap)}</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Principal setor</div><div class="value">${topSetor.s}</div><div class="sub">${fmtCap(topSetor.cap)} em capital</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">MEI médio</div><div class="value">${(avgMeiPct*100).toFixed(0)}%</div><div class="sub">Participação média nos bairros</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Matrizes</div><div class="value">${matrizes.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</div><div class="sub">${(matrizes/totalMGE*100).toFixed(0)}% são sede · ${(totalMGE-matrizes).toLocaleString('pt-BR')} filiais</div></div>`;

  // --- MGE Charts ---
  const setorTop15=MGE_SETOR.slice(0,15);
  makeBar('chart-mge-setor',setorTop15.map(s=>s.s),[{data:setorTop15.map(s=>s.cap),backgroundColor:setorTop15.map(s=>mgeColor(s.cap)),borderRadius:5}],{plugins:{tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>fmtCap(ctx.parsed.x)}}}});
  makeBar('chart-mge-bairro',MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.b),[{data:MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.n),backgroundColor:UI.blue,borderRadius:5}]);

  // --- Populate filters ---
  const selBairro=document.getElementById('mge-bairro-filter');
  const selSetor=document.getElementById('mge-setor-filter');
  const selRegiao=document.getElementById('mge-regiao-filter');
  const selTipo=document.getElementById('mge-tipo-filter');
  const mgeMapSearch=document.getElementById('mge-map-search');
  const bairrosBubble=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[4]))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  bairrosBubble.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;selBairro.appendChild(o)});
  const setores=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[3]))].sort();
  setores.forEach(s=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=s;o.textContent=s;selSetor.appendChild(o)});
  const regioesBubble=[...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>REGION_MAP[r[4]]||'').filter(Boolean))].sort();
  regioesBubble.forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;selRegiao.appendChild(o)});

  // --- MGE Map (choropleth + clustering) ---
  // Build capital lookup for choropleth
  const capByBairro={};
  MGE_BAIRRO.forEach(b=>{capByBairro[b.b]=b.cap});
  const maxBairroCap=Math.max(...MGE_BAIRRO.map(b=>b.cap));
  function choroplethColor(cap){
    if(!cap||cap<=0) return '#F5F3FF';
    const t=Math.log10(cap+1)/Math.log10(maxBairroCap+1);
    const stops=['#F5F3FF','#EDE9FE','#DDD6FE','#C4B5FD','#A78BFA','#8B5CF6','#7C3AED','#6D28D9','#5B21B6'];
    const idx=Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length),stops.length-1);
    return stops[idx];
  }
  if(!MAPS['map-mge']){
    MAPS['map-mge']={};
    const m=L.map('map-mge',{zoomControl:false,scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO',maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-mge'].map=m;
    MAPS['map-mge'].geo=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
      style:function(f){
        const cap=capByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0;
        return {fillColor:choroplethColor(cap),fillOpacity:0.55,color:'#7C3AED',weight:1,opacity:0.4};
      },
      onEachFeature:function(f,layer){
        const d=findData(f.properties.nome);
        const mge=MGE_BAIRRO.find(b=>b.b===f.properties.nome);
        const reg=REGION_MAP[f.properties.nome]||'—';
        const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#888';
        if(d||mge){
          const capStr=mge?fmtCap(mge.cap):'—';
          const nStr=mge?mge.n:0;
          const avgStr=mge?fmtCap(mge.avg):'—';
          layer.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup"><h4>${f.properties.nome}</h4><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${regCol};margin-bottom:6px">${reg}</div><div class="mge-cap">${capStr}</div><div class="mge-meta">${nStr} empresas M/G · capital médio ${avgStr}<br>Pop. ${d?fmt(d.pop,'int'):'—'} · MEI ${d&&d.meiShare!=null?fmt(d.meiShare,'pct'):'—'}</div></div>`,{maxWidth:300});
        }
        layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({weight:2.5,fillOpacity:.7})});
        layer.on('mouseout',function(){MAPS['map-mge'].geo.resetStyle(this)});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    // Cluster layer instead of raw bubbles
    MAPS['map-mge'].clusterLayer=L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius:45,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom:true,
      showCoverageOnHover:false,
      iconCreateFunction:function(cluster){
        const count=cluster.getChildCount();
        let cls='mge-cluster-sm';
        if(count>=50) cls='mge-cluster-lg';
        else if(count>=15) cls='mge-cluster-md';
        return L.divIcon({html:'<div class="mge-cluster '+cls+'">'+count+'</div>',className:'',iconSize:[52,52],iconAnchor:[26,26]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-mge'].geo.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});
  }

  let mgeListVisible=20;
  function getFilteredMGE(){
    const bf=selBairro.value;
    const sf=selSetor.value;
    const rf=selRegiao.value;
    const tf=selTipo.value;
    const sq=(mgeMapSearch.value||'').toLowerCase();
    let filtered=[...MGE_BUBBLES];
    if(bf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[4]===bf);
    if(sf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[3]===sf);
    if(rf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>(REGION_MAP[r[4]]||'')===rf);
    if(tf) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[6]===tf);
    if(sq) filtered=filtered.filter(r=>r[5].toLowerCase().includes(sq));
    return filtered.sort((a,b)=>b[2]-a[2]);
  }

  function renderMGEMap(){
    const cl=MAPS['map-mge'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered=getFilteredMGE();
    filtered.forEach(r=>{
      const [lat,lng,cap,setor,bairro,nome,tipo,cnpj,razao,endereco,cep,dtIni,cnae,nj]=r;
      const reg=REGION_MAP[bairro]||'—';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#888';
      const dotColor=mgeColor(cap);
      const mk=L.marker([lat,lng],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${dotColor};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      const detailRows=[
        cnpj?`<div class="row"><b>CNPJ</b>${cnpj}</div>`:'',
        endereco?`<div class="row"><b>Endereço</b>${endereco}${cep?` · CEP ${cep}`:''}</div>`:''
      ].filter(Boolean).join('');
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup" style="min-width:280px;max-width:340px">
        <h4>${nome}</h4>
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${regCol};margin-bottom:6px">${reg} · ${bairro}</div>
        <div class="mge-cap">${fmtCap(cap)}</div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <span class="mge-tag">${setor}</span>
        </div>
        ${detailRows?`<div class="mge-detail">${detailRows}</div>`:''}
      </div>`,{maxWidth:360});
      cl.addLayer(mk);
    });

    // Sidebar — full empresa list with scroll
    mgeListVisible=20;
    renderMGEList();
  }

  function renderMGEList(){
    const filtered=getFilteredMGE();
    const totalCap=filtered.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[2],0);
    const bf=selBairro.value,sf=selSetor.value,rf=selRegiao.value,tf=selTipo.value;
    const activeFilters=[bf&&`bairro: ${bf}`,sf&&`setor: ${sf}`,rf&&`região: ${rf}`,tf&&(tf==='M'?'Matrizes':'Filiais')].filter(Boolean);
    document.getElementById('mge-analysis-text').innerHTML=`<strong>${filtered.length.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</strong> empresa${filtered.length!==1?'s':''} M/G${activeFilters.length?` <span style="color:var(--brand)">(${activeFilters.join(' · ')})</span>`:''} · capital total <strong>${fmtCap(totalCap)}</strong>`;
    const shown=filtered.slice(0,mgeListVisible);
    const listEl=document.getElementById('mge-top-list');
    listEl.innerHTML=shown.map((r,i)=>{
      const reg=REGION_MAP[r[4]]||'';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#ccc';
      return `<div style="padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-2)">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:10px;min-width:18px">${i+1}</span>
          <span style="flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;color:var(--navy);line-height:1.3">${r[5]}</span>
          <span style="font-weight:500;color:#7C3AED;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap">${fmtCap(r[2])}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="padding-left:24px;margin-top:2px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${regCol};display:inline-block;flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${r[4]}</span>
          <span class="sector-tag" style="font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px">${r[3]}</span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:600;color:${r[6]==='M'?'#7C3AED':'#7c5c00'}">${r[6]==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('')+(mgeListVisible<filtered.length?`<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">Exibindo ${mgeListVisible} de ${filtered.length} — role para ver mais ↓</div>`:'');
  }
  // Scroll-based lazy loading for empresa list
  document.getElementById('mge-top-list').addEventListener('scroll',function(){
    if(this.scrollTop+this.clientHeight>=this.scrollHeight-30){
      const total=getFilteredMGE().length;
      if(mgeListVisible<total){
        mgeListVisible=Math.min(mgeListVisible+20,total);
        renderMGEList();
      }
    }
  });

  function updateFilterUI(){
    const bf=selBairro.value,sf=selSetor.value,rf=selRegiao.value,tf=selTipo.value,sq=mgeMapSearch.value;
    const active=[bf,sf,rf,tf,sq].filter(Boolean).length;
    const clearBtn=document.getElementById('mge-clear-filters');
    const badge=document.getElementById('mge-filter-count');
    clearBtn.style.display=active?'':'none';
    badge.style.display=active?'':'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent=`${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    selBairro.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!bf);
    selSetor.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!sf);
    selRegiao.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!rf);
    selTipo.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!tf);
    mgeMapSearch.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!sq);
    renderMGEMap();
  }
  renderMGEMap();
  selBairro.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selSetor.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selRegiao.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  selTipo.addEventListener('change',updateFilterUI);
  mgeMapSearch.addEventListener('input',updateFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('mge-clear-filters').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    selBairro.value='';selSetor.value='';selRegiao.value='';selTipo.value='';mgeMapSearch.value='';
    updateFilterUI();
  });

  // --- Detailed business table (sortable + searchable + region) ---
  // Populate region filter
  const econRegSel=document.getElementById('econ-table-regiao');
  REGION_ORDER.forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;econRegSel.appendChild(o)});

  let econSortKey='emp';
  let econSortDir=-1;
  const econSortable=['b','emp','mei','meiShare','mpe','mge','coops','regiao'];

  function renderEconTable(){
    const q=(document.getElementById('econ-table-search').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const rf=econRegSel.value;
    let rows=[...DATA].filter(d=>d.emp!=null);
    if(q) rows=rows.filter(d=>d.b.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(rf) rows=rows.filter(d=>(REGION_MAP[d.b]||'')===rf);
    rows.sort((a,b)=>{
      let va,vb;
      if(econSortKey==='regiao'){va=(REGION_MAP[a.b]||'');vb=(REGION_MAP[b.b]||'');}
      else{va=a[econSortKey];vb=b[econSortKey];}
      if(va==null) return 1; if(vb==null) return -1;
      if(typeof va==='string') return econSortDir*va.localeCompare(vb,'pt-BR');
      return econSortDir*(va-vb);
    });
    const countEl=document.getElementById('econ-table-count');
    if(countEl) countEl.textContent=`${rows.length} bairro${rows.length!==1?'s':''}`;
    const colHeaders=[
      {k:'b',label:'Bairro'},{k:'emp',label:'Empresas (2024)'},{k:'mei',label:'MEIs (2024)'},
      {k:'meiShare',label:'% MEI (2024)'},{k:'mpe',label:'Micro/Peq. (2024)'},{k:'mge',label:'Méd./Grd. (2024)'},
      {k:'coops',label:'Coops. (2024)'},{k:'regiao',label:'Região'}
    ];
    document.getElementById('table-econ').innerHTML=`<thead><tr>
      ${colHeaders.map(h=>`<th class="sortable${econSortKey===h.k?' sort-'+(econSortDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-econ-col="${h.k}">${h.label}<span class="sort-icon">${econSortKey===h.k?(econSortDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`).join('')}
      <th>Principal CNAE</th>
    </tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(d=>{
      const reg=REGION_MAP[d.b]||'—';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#ccc';
      const meiHigh=d.meiShare!=null&&d.meiShare>.8;
      const meiMed=d.meiShare!=null&&d.meiShare>.6&&d.meiShare<=.8;
      return `<tr${meiHigh?' class="mei-high"':''}>
        <td>${d.b}</td>
        <td>${fmt(d.emp,'int')}</td>
        <td>${fmt(d.mei,'int')}</td>
        <td style="color:${meiHigh?'#B07C20':meiMed?'#7C3AED':'var(--text)'};font-weight:500">${d.meiShare!=null?fmt(d.meiShare,'pct'):'—'}</td>
        <td>${fmt(d.mpe,'int')}</td>
        <td>${fmt(d.mge,'int')}</td>
        <td>${fmt(d.coops,'int')}</td>
        <td><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap"><span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${regCol};flex-shrink:0"></span>${reg}</span></td>
        <td style="font-size:11px;max-width:200px;color:var(--muted)">${d.cn1||'—'}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('')}</tbody>`;
    document.querySelectorAll('#table-econ th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const col=th.dataset.econCol;
        if(econSortKey===col) econSortDir*=-1;
        else{econSortKey=col;econSortDir=-1;}
        renderEconTable();
      });
    });
  }
  renderEconTable();
  document.getElementById('econ-table-search').addEventListener('input',renderEconTable);
  econRegSel.addEventListener('change',renderEconTable);

  // Populate MGE table filters
  const mgeRegSel = document.getElementById('mge-table-regiao');
  const mgeSetSel = document.getElementById('mge-table-setor');
  if(mgeRegSel && mgeRegSel.options.length <= 1){
    Object.keys(REGION_COLORS).forEach(r => { const o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=r; o.textContent=r; mgeRegSel.appendChild(o); });
  }
  if(mgeSetSel && mgeSetSel.options.length <= 1){
    const setores = [...new Set(MGE_BUBBLES.map(r=>r[3]))].sort();
    setores.forEach(s => { const o=document.createElement('option'); o.value=s; o.textContent=s; mgeSetSel.appendChild(o); });
  }
  renderMGETable();
  ['mge-table-search','mge-table-regiao','mge-table-setor','mge-table-tipo'].forEach(id => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change', renderMGETable);
  });

  // --- Mapa do setor alimentício ---
  initAlimentMap();
}

const ALIMENT_CAT = {
  R: {label:'Restaurantes & Lanchonetes', color:'#DC2626', icon:'🍴'},
  H: {label:'Hortifruti',                  color:'#16A34A', icon:'🥬'},
  A: {label:'Atacado especializado',       color:'#0D9488', icon:'📦'},
  S: {label:'Hiper & Supermercados',       color:'#7C3AED', icon:'🛒'}
};

function alimentMarkerIcon(cat){
  const c = ALIMENT_CAT[cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
  return L.divIcon({
    className:'aliment-marker',
    html:`<div class="am-dot" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</div>`,
    iconSize:[22,22],
    iconAnchor:[11,11]
  });
}

function initAlimentMap(){
  if(typeof ALIMENT_POINTS==='undefined' || !document.getElementById('map-aliment')) return;

  // 1) Counts per bairro (total, ignoring filters) — used for choropleth
  const totalByBairro = {};
  ALIMENT_POINTS.forEach(p=>{ totalByBairro[p.b] = (totalByBairro[p.b]||0) + 1; });
  const maxBairroAliment = Math.max(...Object.values(totalByBairro), 1);

  function alimentChoropleth(n){
    if(!n) return '#F5F3FF';
    const t = Math.log10(n+1) / Math.log10(maxBairroAliment+1);
    const stops = ['#F5F3FF','#EDE9FE','#DDD6FE','#C4B5FD','#A78BFA','#8B5CF6','#7C3AED','#6D28D9'];
    return stops[Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length), stops.length-1)];
  }

  // 2) Populate region and bairro filters
  const selCat = document.getElementById('aliment-cat-filter');
  const selTipo = document.getElementById('aliment-tipo-filter');
  const selRegiao = document.getElementById('aliment-regiao-filter');
  const selBairro = document.getElementById('aliment-bairro-filter');
  const inpSearch = document.getElementById('aliment-map-search');
  const btnClear = document.getElementById('aliment-clear-filters');
  const badge = document.getElementById('aliment-filter-count');
  const txtAnalysis = document.getElementById('aliment-analysis-text');
  const listEl = document.getElementById('aliment-top-list');

  // Populate region filter
  REGION_ORDER.forEach(r=>{
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = r; opt.textContent = r;
    selRegiao.appendChild(opt);
  });

  const allAlimentBairros = [...new Set(ALIMENT_POINTS.map(p=>p.b))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  function populateAlimentBairros(bairroList){
    selBairro.innerHTML = '<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>';
    bairroList.forEach(b=>{
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = b; opt.textContent = `${b} (${totalByBairro[b]||0})`;
      selBairro.appendChild(opt);
    });
  }
  populateAlimentBairros(allAlimentBairros);

  // 3) Build map
  if(!MAPS['map-aliment']){
    MAPS['map-aliment'] = {};
    const m = L.map('map-aliment', {zoomControl:false, scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO', maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-aliment'].map = m;

    MAPS['map-aliment'].geo = L.geoJSON(GEO, {
      style: f => ({
        fillColor: alimentChoropleth(totalByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0),
        fillOpacity: 0.55, color:'#7C3AED', weight:1, opacity:0.4
      }),
      onEachFeature: (f, layer) => {
        const n = totalByBairro[f.properties.nome] || 0;
        if(n>0) layer.bindTooltip(`<strong>${f.properties.nome}</strong><br>${n} estabelecimento${n>1?'s':''} alimentício${n>1?'s':''}`, {sticky:true});
        layer.on('mouseover', function(){ this.setStyle({weight:2.5, fillOpacity:0.72}); });
        layer.on('mouseout',  function(){ MAPS['map-aliment'].geo.resetStyle(this); });
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    MAPS['map-aliment'].clusterLayer = L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius: 45,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom: true,
      showCoverageOnHover: false,
      iconCreateFunction: function(cluster){
        const count = cluster.getChildCount();
        let cls = 'mge-cluster-sm';
        if(count >= 80) cls = 'mge-cluster-lg';
        else if(count >= 25) cls = 'mge-cluster-md';
        return L.divIcon({html:'<div class="mge-cluster '+cls+'">'+count+'</div>', className:'', iconSize:[52,52], iconAnchor:[26,26]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-aliment'].geo.getBounds(), {padding:[20,20]});
  }

  function getFilteredAliment(){
    const cf = selCat.value;
    const tf = selTipo.value;
    const rf = selRegiao.value;
    const bf = selBairro.value;
    const sq = (inpSearch.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    let out = ALIMENT_POINTS;
    if(cf) out = out.filter(p=>p.cat===cf);
    if(tf) out = out.filter(p=>p.tipo===tf);
    if(rf) out = out.filter(p=>(REGION_MAP[p.b]||'')===rf);
    if(bf) out = out.filter(p=>p.b===bf);
    if(sq) out = out.filter(p=>(p.n||'').toLowerCase().includes(sq) || (p.rf||'').toLowerCase().includes(sq) || (p.id||'').includes(sq));
    return out;
  }

  function fmtCnpj(c){
    if(!c || c.length!==14) return c;
    return `${c.slice(0,2)}.${c.slice(2,5)}.${c.slice(5,8)}/${c.slice(8,12)}-${c.slice(12)}`;
  }

  function renderAlimentMap(){
    const cl = MAPS['map-aliment'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered = getFilteredAliment();

    filtered.forEach(p=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[p.cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
      const mk = L.marker([p.lat, p.lon], {icon: alimentMarkerIcon(p.cat)});
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup" style="min-width:260px;max-width:320px">
        <h4>${p.n}</h4>
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${c.color};margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px">
          <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color};width:14px;height:14px;font-size:8px">${c.icon}</span>${c.label}
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap">
          <span class="tipo-badge ${p.tipo==='M'?'tipo-m':'tipo-f'}">${p.tipo==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span>
          <span class="sector-tag">${p.b}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="mge-detail">
          <div class="row"><b>CNPJ</b>${fmtCnpj(p.id)}</div>
          <div class="row"><b>Razão social</b>${p.rf||'—'}</div>
          <div class="row"><b>CNAE</b>${p.cnae} · ${p.desc||'—'}</div>
          ${p.addr?`<div class="row"><b>Endereço cadastral</b>${p.addr}${p.cep?` · CEP ${p.cep}`:''}</div>`:''}
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:10px;color:#94a3b8;font-style:italic">Posição aproximada (centroide do bairro com jitter)</div>
      </div>`, {maxWidth:340});
      cl.addLayer(mk);
    });

    // Update side panel
    const total = filtered.length;
    const byCat = {R:0, H:0, A:0, S:0};
    filtered.forEach(p=>{ byCat[p.cat] = (byCat[p.cat]||0) + 1; });
    const matrizes = filtered.filter(p=>p.tipo==='M').length;
    txtAnalysis.innerHTML = `<strong>${total.toLocaleString('pt-BR')}</strong> estabelecimento${total!==1?'s':''} no filtro · ${matrizes} matriz${matrizes!==1?'es':''} e ${total-matrizes} filia${total-matrizes!==1?'is':'l'}`;

    const catRows = ['R','H','A','S'].filter(k=>byCat[k]>0).map(k=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[k];
      const pct = total ? ((byCat[k]/total)*100).toFixed(0) : 0;
      return `<div class="aliment-row" style="background:#FAFBFD">
        <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</span>
        <span class="nm">${c.label}</span>
        <span class="meta"><strong>${byCat[k]}</strong> · ${pct}%</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    const listVisible = 60;
    const sliced = filtered.slice(0, listVisible);
    const rows = sliced.map(p=>{
      const c = ALIMENT_CAT[p.cat] || ALIMENT_CAT.R;
      return `<div class="aliment-row" title="${p.rf||p.n}">
        <span class="aliment-cat-icon" style="background:${c.color}">${c.icon}</span>
        <span class="nm">${p.n}</span>
        <span class="meta">${p.b}</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
    const more = filtered.length > listVisible ? `<div style="padding:8px;text-align:center;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);font-style:italic">+ ${filtered.length-listVisible} estabelecimentos não exibidos · refine o filtro</div>` : '';
    listEl.innerHTML = `<div style="margin-bottom:8px">${catRows}</div><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;padding:6px 8px 4px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line-2)">Lista (${Math.min(listVisible,filtered.length)} de ${filtered.length})</div>${rows}${more}`;

    // Filter badge
    const active = (selCat.value?1:0) + (selTipo.value?1:0) + (selRegiao.value?1:0) + (selBairro.value?1:0) + (inpSearch.value?1:0);
    badge.style.display = active ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    btnClear.style.display = active ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent = `${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    [selCat,selTipo,selRegiao,selBairro].forEach(s=>s.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!s.value));
    inpSearch.classList.toggle('active-filter', !!inpSearch.value);
  }

  selCat.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  selTipo.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  selRegiao.addEventListener('change', ()=>{
    const reg = selRegiao.value;
    const filteredBairros = reg ? allAlimentBairros.filter(b=>(REGION_MAP[b]||'')===reg) : allAlimentBairros;
    populateAlimentBairros(filteredBairros);
    renderAlimentMap();
  });
  selBairro.addEventListener('change', renderAlimentMap);
  inpSearch.addEventListener('input', renderAlimentMap);
  btnClear.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
    selCat.value=''; selTipo.value=''; selRegiao.value=''; selBairro.value=''; inpSearch.value='';
    populateAlimentBairros(allAlimentBairros);
    renderAlimentMap();
  });

  renderAlimentMap();
  setTimeout(()=>{ try{ MAPS['map-aliment'].map.invalidateSize(); }catch(e){} }, 80);
}

// Full MGE company list table
let mgeTableSort = 'cap', mgeTableDir = -1;
function renderMGETable() {
  const q = (document.getElementById('mge-table-search').value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const rf = document.getElementById('mge-table-regiao').value;
  const sf = document.getElementById('mge-table-setor').value;
  const tf = document.getElementById('mge-table-tipo').value;

  let rows = [...MGE_BUBBLES];
  if(q) rows = rows.filter(r => r[5].toLowerCase().includes(q) || r[4].toLowerCase().includes(q));
  if(rf) rows = rows.filter(r => (REGION_MAP[r[4]]||'') === rf);
  if(sf) rows = rows.filter(r => r[3] === sf);
  if(tf) rows = rows.filter(r => r[6] === tf);

  const sortIdx = {nome:5, cap:2, setor:3, bairro:4, tipo:6}[mgeTableSort] || 2;
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    let av, bv;
    if(mgeTableSort === 'regiao'){
      av = (REGION_MAP[a[4]]||'').toLowerCase();
      bv = (REGION_MAP[b[4]]||'').toLowerCase();
    } else {
      av = typeof a[sortIdx] === 'string' ? a[sortIdx].toLowerCase() : a[sortIdx];
      bv = typeof b[sortIdx] === 'string' ? b[sortIdx].toLowerCase() : b[sortIdx];
    }
    if(typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') return mgeTableDir === -1 ? bv - av : av - bv;
    return mgeTableDir === -1 ? String(bv).localeCompare(String(av),'pt') : String(av).localeCompare(String(bv),'pt');
  });

  const countEl = document.getElementById('mge-table-count');
  if(countEl) countEl.textContent = rows.length + ' de ' + MGE_BUBBLES.length + ' empresas';

  const cols = [
    {k:'nome',label:'Empresa'},
    {k:'cap',label:'Capital Social'},
    {k:'setor',label:'Setor'},
    {k:'bairro',label:'Bairro'},
    {k:'regiao',label:'Região'},
    {k:'tipo',label:'Tipo'}
  ];

  const tbl = document.getElementById('table-mge-list');
  tbl.innerHTML = `<thead><tr>${cols.map(c =>
    `<th class="sortable${mgeTableSort===c.k?' sort-'+(mgeTableDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-mge-col="${c.k}">${c.label}<span class="sort-icon">${mgeTableSort===c.k?(mgeTableDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`
  ).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(0,500).map((r,i) => {
    const reg = REGION_MAP[r[4]] || '';
    const regCol = REGION_COLORS[reg] || '#999';
    return `<tr>
      <td style="font-weight:600;max-width:280px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${r[5]}">${r[5]}</td>
      <td data-sort-value="${r[2]}" style="white-space:nowrap">${fmtCap(r[2])}</td>
      <td><span class="sector-tag">${r[3]}</span></td>
      <td>${r[4]}</td>
      <td><span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap"><span class="region-dot" style="background:${regCol}"></span>${reg}</span></td>
      <td><span class="tipo-badge ${r[6]==='M'?'tipo-m':'tipo-f'}">${r[6]==='M'?'Matriz':'Filial'}</span></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('')}${rows.length>500?`<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;padding:12px">Mostrando 500 de ${rows.length} empresas. Use os filtros para refinar.</td></tr>`:''}</tbody>`;

  document.querySelectorAll('#table-mge-list th.sortable').forEach(th => {
    th.onclick = () => {
      const col = th.dataset.mgeCol;
      if(mgeTableSort === col) mgeTableDir *= -1;
      else { mgeTableSort = col; mgeTableDir = -1; }
      renderMGETable();
    };
  });
}

function initOsc(){
  const totalOsc=OSCS.length;
  const areaCounts={};
  OSCS.forEach(o=>{areaCounts[o.a]=(areaCounts[o.a]||0)+1;});
  const bairroCounts={};
  OSCS.forEach(o=>{bairroCounts[o.bairro]=(bairroCounts[o.bairro]||0)+1;});
  const totalBairros=Object.keys(bairroCounts).length;
  const bairrosComOsc=Object.keys(bairroCounts).filter(b=>bairroCounts[b]>0).length;
  const topArea=Object.entries(areaCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];
  const topBairro=Object.entries(bairroCounts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0];
  const religiosas=OSCS.filter(o=>o.a==='Religiao').length;

  document.getElementById('kpi-osc').innerHTML=`
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Total OSCs</div><div class="value">${totalOsc}</div><div class="sub">organizações ativas</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Bairros cobertos</div><div class="value">${bairroCounts ? Object.keys(bairroCounts).length : 0}</div><div class="sub">de 44 bairros</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Área predominante</div><div class="value" style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.3">${AREA_LABELS[topArea[0]]||topArea[0]}</div><div class="sub">${topArea[1]} entidades (${Math.round(topArea[1]/totalOsc*100)}%)</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Bairro com mais OSCs</div><div class="value" style="font-size:16px;line-height:1.3">${topBairro[0]}</div><div class="sub">${topBairro[1]} organizações</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Entidades religiosas</div><div class="value">${religiosas}</div><div class="sub">${Math.round(religiosas/totalOsc*100)}% do total</div></div>
    <div class="kpi"><div class="label">Áreas de atuação</div><div class="value">${Object.keys(areaCounts).length}</div><div class="sub">categorias distintas</div></div>
  `;

  // Charts removed — now using network visualization instead

  // --- Populate filters ---
  const selBairro=document.getElementById('osc-bairro-filter');
  const selRegiao=document.getElementById('osc-regiao-filter');
  const selArea=document.getElementById('osc-area-filter');
  const oscBairros=[...new Set(OSCS.map(o=>o.bairro))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,'pt-BR'));
  oscBairros.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;selBairro.appendChild(o)});
  const oscRegioes=[...new Set(OSCS.map(o=>REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'').filter(Boolean))].sort();
  oscRegioes.forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;selRegiao.appendChild(o)});
  const oscAreas=[...new Set(OSCS.map(o=>o.a))].sort();
  oscAreas.forEach(a=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=a;o.textContent=AREA_LABELS[a]||a;selArea.appendChild(o)});

  // Build OSC count per bairro for choropleth
  const oscByBairro={};
  OSCS.forEach(o=>{oscByBairro[o.bairro]=(oscByBairro[o.bairro]||0)+1;});
  const maxOscBairro=Math.max(...Object.values(oscByBairro));
  function oscChoroplethColor(n){
    if(!n||n<=0) return '#F5F3FF';
    const t=Math.log10(n+1)/Math.log10(maxOscBairro+1);
    const stops=['#F5F3FF','#EDE9FE','#DDD6FE','#C4B5FD','#A78BFA','#8B5CF6','#7C3AED','#6D28D9','#5B21B6'];
    return stops[Math.min(Math.floor(t*stops.length),stops.length-1)];
  }

  // Map
  if(!MAPS['map-osc']){
    MAPS['map-osc']={};
    const m=L.map('map-osc',{zoomControl:false,scrollWheelZoom:true});
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/light_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png',{attribution:'© OSM · CARTO',maxZoom:18}).addTo(m);
    L.control.zoom({position:'bottomleft'}).addTo(m);
    MAPS['map-osc'].map=m;

    // GeoJSON choropleth by OSC density
    MAPS['map-osc'].geo=L.geoJSON(GEO,{
      style:function(f){
        const n=oscByBairro[f.properties.nome]||0;
        return {fillColor:oscChoroplethColor(n),fillOpacity:0.55,color:'#7C3AED',weight:1,opacity:0.4};
      },
      onEachFeature:function(f,layer){
        const nome=f.properties.nome;
        const n=oscByBairro[nome]||0;
        const reg=REGION_MAP[nome]||'—';
        const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#888';
        const d=findData(nome);
        layer.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup"><h4>${nome}</h4><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${regCol};margin-bottom:6px">${reg}</div><div class="mge-cap">${n} OSCs</div><div class="mge-meta">Pop. ${d?fmt(d.pop,'int'):'—'}</div></div>`,{maxWidth:300});
        layer.on('mouseover',function(){this.setStyle({weight:2.5,fillOpacity:.7})});
        layer.on('mouseout',function(){MAPS['map-osc'].geo.resetStyle(this)});
      }
    }).addTo(m);

    // MarkerCluster
    MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer=L.markerClusterGroup({
      maxClusterRadius:45,
      spiderfyOnMaxZoom:true,
      showCoverageOnHover:false,
      iconCreateFunction:function(cluster){
        const count=cluster.getChildCount();
        let cls='mge-cluster-sm';
        if(count>=50) cls='mge-cluster-lg';
        else if(count>=15) cls='mge-cluster-md';
        return L.divIcon({html:'<div class="mge-cluster '+cls+'">'+count+'</div>',className:'',iconSize:[52,52],iconAnchor:[26,26]});
      }
    }).addTo(m);
    const cluster=MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer;
    OSCS.forEach(o=>{
      const color=AREA_COLORS[o.a]||'#999';
      const areaLabel=AREA_LABELS[o.a]||o.a;
      const reg=REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'—';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#888';
      const mk=L.marker([o.lat,o.lng],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      mk._oscData=o;
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup">
        <h4>${o.n}</h4>
        ${o.f?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-bottom:4px">'+o.f+'</div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${regCol};margin-bottom:6px">${reg} · ${o.bairro}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0">
          <span class="mge-tag" style="background:${color};color:#fff">${areaLabel}</span>
        </div>
        ${o.fund?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-top:6px">Fundação: <strong>'+o.fund+'</strong></div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#999;margin-top:4px;border-top:1px solid #eee;padding-top:4px">${o.end}</div>
      </div>`,{maxWidth:340});
      cluster.addLayer(mk);
    });

    m.fitBounds(MAPS['map-osc'].geo.getBounds(),{padding:[20,20]});
  }

  // --- Filter + render logic (like Economia tab) ---
  let oscListVisible=20;

  function getFilteredOSC(){
    const bf=selBairro.value;
    const rf=selRegiao.value;
    const af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    let filtered=[...OSCS];
    if(bf) filtered=filtered.filter(o=>o.bairro===bf);
    if(rf) filtered=filtered.filter(o=>(REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'')===rf);
    if(af) filtered=filtered.filter(o=>o.a===af);
    if(sf) filtered=filtered.filter(o=>o.n.toLowerCase().includes(sf)||(o.f&&o.f.toLowerCase().includes(sf)));
    return filtered;
  }

  function renderOscMap(){
    const cl=MAPS['map-osc'].clusterLayer;
    cl.clearLayers();
    const filtered=getFilteredOSC();
    filtered.forEach(o=>{
      const color=AREA_COLORS[o.a]||'#999';
      const areaLabel=AREA_LABELS[o.a]||o.a;
      const reg=REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'—';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#888';
      const mk=L.marker([o.lat,o.lng],{
        icon:L.divIcon({
          className:'',
          html:`<div style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:${color};border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.25)"></div>`,
          iconSize:[10,10],iconAnchor:[5,5]
        })
      });
      mk.bindPopup(`<div class="mge-popup">
        <h4>${o.n}</h4>
        ${o.f?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-bottom:4px">'+o.f+'</div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${regCol};margin-bottom:6px">${reg} · ${o.bairro}</div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0">
          <span class="mge-tag" style="background:${color};color:#fff">${areaLabel}</span>
        </div>
        ${o.fund?'<div style="font-size:11px;color:#5C6E7E;margin-top:6px">Fundação: <strong>'+o.fund+'</strong></div>':''}
        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#999;margin-top:4px;border-top:1px solid #eee;padding-top:4px">${o.end}</div>
      </div>`,{maxWidth:340});
      cl.addLayer(mk);
    });

    document.getElementById('osc-map-count').textContent=filtered.length+' OSCs';
    oscListVisible=20;
    renderOscList();
  }

  function renderOscList(){
    const filtered=getFilteredOSC();
    const bf=selBairro.value,rf=selRegiao.value,af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    const activeFilters=[bf&&`bairro: ${bf}`,rf&&`região: ${rf}`,af&&`área: ${AREA_LABELS[af]||af}`,sf&&`busca: "${sf}"`].filter(Boolean);

    // Count areas in filtered
    const filteredAreas={};
    filtered.forEach(o=>{filteredAreas[o.a]=(filteredAreas[o.a]||0)+1;});
    const areaBreakdown=Object.entries(filteredAreas).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,3).map(([k,v])=>`${AREA_LABELS[k]||k}: ${v}`).join(' · ');

    document.getElementById('osc-analysis-text').innerHTML=`<strong>${filtered.length}</strong> OSC${filtered.length!==1?'s':''}${activeFilters.length?` <span style="color:var(--brand)">(${activeFilters.join(' · ')})</span>`:''}<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${areaBreakdown}</span>`;

    const shown=filtered.slice(0,oscListVisible);
    const listEl=document.getElementById('osc-top-list');
    listEl.innerHTML=shown.map((o,i)=>{
      const reg=REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'';
      const regCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#ccc';
      const areaCol=AREA_COLORS[o.a]||'#999';
      return `<div style="padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-2)">
        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:10px;min-width:18px">${i+1}</span>
          <span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${areaCol};flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="flex:1;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;color:var(--navy);line-height:1.3">${o.f||o.n}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="padding-left:32px;margin-top:2px;display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
          <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${regCol};display:inline-block;flex-shrink:0"></span>
          <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted)">${o.bairro}</span>
          <span class="sector-tag" style="font-size:9px;padding:1px 5px">${AREA_LABELS[o.a]||o.a}</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('')+(oscListVisible<filtered.length?`<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px;font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">Exibindo ${oscListVisible} de ${filtered.length} — role para ver mais ↓</div>`:'');
  }

  // Lazy scroll
  document.getElementById('osc-top-list').addEventListener('scroll',function(){
    if(this.scrollTop+this.clientHeight>=this.scrollHeight-30){
      const total=getFilteredOSC().length;
      if(oscListVisible<total){
        oscListVisible=Math.min(oscListVisible+20,total);
        renderOscList();
      }
    }
  });

  function updateOscFilterUI(){
    const bf=selBairro.value,rf=selRegiao.value,af=selArea.value;
    const sf=(document.getElementById('osc-search').value||'').toLowerCase();
    const active=[bf,rf,af,sf].filter(Boolean).length;
    const clearBtn=document.getElementById('osc-clear-filters');
    const badge=document.getElementById('osc-filter-count');
    clearBtn.style.display=active?'':'none';
    badge.style.display=active?'':'none';
    if(active) badge.textContent=`${active} filtro${active>1?'s':''}`;
    selBairro.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!bf);
    selRegiao.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!rf);
    selArea.classList.toggle('active-filter',!!af);
    renderOscMap();
  }

  renderOscMap();
  selBairro.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  selRegiao.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  selArea.addEventListener('change',updateOscFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('osc-search').addEventListener('input',updateOscFilterUI);
  document.getElementById('osc-clear-filters').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    selBairro.value='';selRegiao.value='';selArea.value='';
    document.getElementById('osc-search').value='';
    updateOscFilterUI();
  });

  setTimeout(()=>{if(MAPS['map-osc']&&MAPS['map-osc'].map)MAPS['map-osc'].map.invalidateSize();},200);

  // ── OSC charts: por área e por bairro ──
  const areaCountsChart={};
  OSCS.forEach(o=>{if(o.a&&o.a!=='Sem classificação')areaCountsChart[o.a]=(areaCountsChart[o.a]||0)+1;});
  const areaEntries=Object.entries(areaCountsChart).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  if(areaEntries.length){
    destroyChart('chart-osc-area');
    const cArea=document.getElementById('chart-osc-area');
    if(cArea){
      const h=Math.max(areaEntries.length*28,200);
      cArea.style.height=h+'px';
      cArea.parentElement.style.height='auto';
      CHARTS['chart-osc-area']=new Chart(cArea,{type:'bar',data:{labels:areaEntries.map(e=>e[0].length>40?e[0].slice(0,40)+'…':e[0]),datasets:[{data:areaEntries.map(e=>e[1]),backgroundColor:'rgba(124,58,237,.7)',borderRadius:3}]},options:{indexAxis:'y',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.raw+' OSCs'}}},scales:{x:{grid:{display:false}},y:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{font:{size:11},autoSkip:false,maxTicksLimit:areaEntries.length}}}}});
    }
  }
  const bairroCountsChart={};
  OSCS.forEach(o=>{bairroCountsChart[o.bairro]=(bairroCountsChart[o.bairro]||0)+1;});
  const bairroEntries=Object.entries(bairroCountsChart).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,25);
  if(bairroEntries.length){
    destroyChart('chart-osc-bairro');
    const cBairro=document.getElementById('chart-osc-bairro');
    if(cBairro){
      const h=Math.max(bairroEntries.length*28,200);
      cBairro.style.height=h+'px';
      cBairro.parentElement.style.height='auto';
      CHARTS['chart-osc-bairro']=new Chart(cBairro,{type:'bar',data:{labels:bairroEntries.map(e=>e[0]),datasets:[{data:bairroEntries.map(e=>e[1]),backgroundColor:bairroEntries.map(e=>{const r=REGION_MAP[e[0]]||'';return REGION_COLORS[r]||'rgba(124,58,237,.5)';}),borderRadius:3}]},options:{indexAxis:'y',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:ctx=>ctx.raw+' OSCs'}}},scales:{x:{grid:{display:false}},y:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{font:{size:11},autoSkip:false,maxTicksLimit:bairroEntries.length}}}}});
    }
  }

  // ── Tabela completa de OSCs ──
  let oscTableSort='nome',oscTableDir=-1;
  function renderOscTable(){
    const q=(document.getElementById('osc-table-search').value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
    const rf=document.getElementById('osc-table-regiao').value;
    const af=document.getElementById('osc-table-area').value;
    let rows=[...OSCS];
    if(q) rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.n||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(r.bairro||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(rf) rows=rows.filter(r=>(REGION_MAP[r.bairro]||'')===rf);
    if(af) rows=rows.filter(r=>r.a===af);
    rows.sort((a,b)=>{
      let av,bv;
      if(oscTableSort==='nome'){av=(a.n||'').toLowerCase();bv=(b.n||'').toLowerCase();}
      else if(oscTableSort==='bairro'){av=(a.bairro||'').toLowerCase();bv=(b.bairro||'').toLowerCase();}
      else if(oscTableSort==='area'){av=(a.a||'');bv=(b.a||'');}
      else if(oscTableSort==='regiao'){av=(REGION_MAP[a.bairro]||'').toLowerCase();bv=(REGION_MAP[b.bairro]||'').toLowerCase();}
      else{av=a[oscTableSort]||0;bv=b[oscTableSort]||0;}
      if(typeof av==='number'&&typeof bv==='number') return oscTableDir===-1?bv-av:av-bv;
      return oscTableDir===-1?String(bv).localeCompare(String(av),'pt'):String(av).localeCompare(String(bv),'pt');
    });
    const countEl=document.getElementById('osc-table-count');
    if(countEl) countEl.textContent=rows.length+' de '+OSCS.length+' organizações';
    const cols=[{k:'nome',label:'Organização'},{k:'bairro',label:'Bairro'},{k:'area',label:'Área de atuação'},{k:'regiao',label:'Região'}];
    const tbl=document.getElementById('table-osc-list');
    if(!tbl) return;
    tbl.innerHTML=`<thead><tr>${cols.map(c=>`<th class="sortable${oscTableSort===c.k?' sort-'+(oscTableDir===1?'asc':'desc'):''}" data-osc-col="${c.k}">${c.label}<span class="sort-icon">${oscTableSort===c.k?(oscTableDir===1?'↑':'↓'):'↕'}</span></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.slice(0,500).map(r=>{
      const reg=REGION_MAP[r.bairro]||'';
      const rCol=REGION_COLORS[reg]||'#999';
      return `<tr><td style="font-weight:600;max-width:300px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="${r.n||''}">${r.n||'—'}</td><td>${r.bairro||'—'}</td><td><span class="tag" style="margin:1px 2px">${r.a||'—'}</span></td><td><span class="region-dot" style="background:${rCol};margin-right:4px"></span>${reg||'—'}</td></tr>`;
    }).join('')}${rows.length>500?`<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;padding:12px">Mostrando 500 de ${rows.length} organizações.</td></tr>`:''}</tbody>`;
    document.querySelectorAll('#table-osc-list th.sortable').forEach(th=>{
      th.onclick=()=>{
        const col=th.dataset.oscCol;
        if(oscTableSort===col) oscTableDir*=-1;
        else{oscTableSort=col;oscTableDir=-1;}
        renderOscTable();
      };
    });
  }
  // Populate filters
  const oscTableRegSel=document.getElementById('osc-table-regiao');
  if(oscTableRegSel&&oscTableRegSel.options.length<=1){
    Object.keys(REGION_COLORS).forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;oscTableRegSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  const oscTableAreaSel=document.getElementById('osc-table-area');
  if(oscTableAreaSel&&oscTableAreaSel.options.length<=1){
    const areas=[...new Set(OSCS.map(o=>o.a))].filter(Boolean).sort();
    areas.forEach(a=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=a;o.textContent=a;oscTableAreaSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  renderOscTable();
  ['osc-table-search','osc-table-regiao','osc-table-area'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(el) el.addEventListener(el.tagName==='INPUT'?'input':'change',renderOscTable);
  });
}

/* ── Rede de afinidade — CEASA Mais Nutrição ──────────────────────── */
function initNetwork(){
  const CEASA_BAIRRO='Pajuçara';
  const CEASA_REGION='Zona Leste';
  const FOCUS_AREAS=['Assistencia social','Saude','Educacao e pesquisa'];
  const RELATED_AREAS=['Desenvolvimento e defesa de direitos e interesses'];
  const CEASA_NEIGHBORS=['Pajuçara','Distrito Industrial do Ceará','Jardim Bandeirante',
    'Arronches','Jardim Paraíso','Boa Esperança','Parque Progresso','Parque Tropical','Alto da Bonança'];

  const anchor={id:'ceasa',name:'CEASA Mais Nutrição',shortName:'CEASA',area:'Assistencia social',
    bairro:CEASA_BAIRRO,region:CEASA_REGION,isAnchor:true,
    focus:FOCUS_AREAS,related:RELATED_AREAS,
    desc:'Programa da Central de Abastecimento do Ceará (CEASA/CE) voltado à segurança alimentar e combate ao desperdício. Recupera alimentos excedentes e os distribui via OSCs a famílias em situação de vulnerabilidade no entorno de Pajuçara e região, conectando produção, distribuição e assistência social.'};

  const scored=OSCS.map((o,i)=>{
    let thematic=0,geographic=0;
    if(FOCUS_AREAS.includes(o.a)) thematic=3;
    else if(RELATED_AREAS.includes(o.a)) thematic=1;
    const reg=REGION_MAP[o.bairro]||'';
    if(o.bairro===CEASA_BAIRRO) geographic=3;
    else if(CEASA_NEIGHBORS.includes(o.bairro)) geographic=2;
    else if(reg===CEASA_REGION) geographic=1;
    return{idx:i,...o,region:reg,thematic,geographic,score:thematic+geographic};
  }).filter(o=>o.score>=2);

  const nodes=[anchor,...scored.map(o=>({id:'osc-'+o.idx,name:o.n,shortName:o.f||o.n.slice(0,28),
    fantasia:o.f,end:o.end,fund:o.fund,
    area:o.a,bairro:o.bairro,region:o.region,score:o.score,thematic:o.thematic,geographic:o.geographic}))];

  const edges=[];
  scored.forEach(o=>{
    const strength=o.score>=5?'strong':o.score>=3?'medium':'light';
    edges.push({source:'ceasa',target:'osc-'+o.idx,strength,value:o.score});
  });
  for(let i=0;i<scored.length;i++){
    for(let j=i+1;j<scored.length;j++){
      const a=scored[i],b=scored[j];
      if(a.a===b.a && (a.bairro===b.bairro || (a.region===b.region && a.region!=='')))
        edges.push({source:'osc-'+a.idx,target:'osc-'+b.idx,strength:'peer',value:1});
    }
  }

  // Stats
  const areaCount={};
  scored.forEach(o=>{areaCount[o.a]=(areaCount[o.a]||0)+1});
  const strongCount=edges.filter(e=>e.strength==='strong').length;
  const peerCount=edges.filter(e=>e.strength==='peer').length;
  document.getElementById('network-stats').innerHTML=[
    {label:'OSCs conectadas',value:scored.length,sub:'Por afinidade temática e/ou geográfica'},
    {label:'Conexões fortes',value:strongCount,sub:'Alta afinidade temática + proximidade'},
    {label:'Conexões entre pares',value:peerCount,sub:'OSCs conectadas entre si (área + território)'},
    {label:'Áreas representadas',value:Object.keys(areaCount).length,sub:Object.entries(areaCount).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([k,v])=>(AREA_LABELS[k]||k)+': '+v).join(' · ')}
  ].map(c=>`<div class="analysis-stat"><div class="analysis-stat-label">${c.label}</div><div class="analysis-stat-value">${c.value}</div><div class="analysis-stat-sub">${c.sub}</div></div>`).join('');

  // Region + Bairro filters
  const bairroSel=document.getElementById('net-bairro-filter');
  const regiaoSel=document.getElementById('net-regiao-filter');
  const regioes=[...new Set(scored.map(o=>o.region).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  regioes.forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;regiaoSel.appendChild(o)});
  const allBairros=[...new Set(scored.map(o=>o.bairro))].sort();
  function populateBairros(region){
    bairroSel.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>';
    const list=region?allBairros.filter(b=>(REGION_MAP[b]||'')===region):allBairros;
    list.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;bairroSel.appendChild(o)});
  }
  populateBairros('');
  regiaoSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{populateBairros(regiaoSel.value);bairroSel.value='';applyFilters();});

  // Degree buttons inside graph
  let activeDeg='all';
  document.querySelectorAll('#net-degree-bar .net-deg-btn').forEach(btn=>{
    btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      document.querySelectorAll('#net-degree-bar .net-deg-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      activeDeg=btn.dataset.deg;
      applyFilters();
    });
  });

  // Clickable legend — toggling areas
  const legendAreas=[...new Set(scored.map(o=>o.a))];
  const hiddenAreas=new Set();
  const legendEl=document.getElementById('network-legend');
  function renderLegend(){
    const sorted=legendAreas.slice().sort((a,b)=>(areaCount[b]||0)-(areaCount[a]||0));
    legendEl.innerHTML=
      `<div class="net-leg"><div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:#E63946"></div><span class="net-leg-name" style="font-weight:600">CEASA (âncora)</span></div>`+
      sorted.map(a=>{
        const off=hiddenAreas.has(a);
        const cnt=areaCount[a]||0;
        return `<div class="net-leg net-leg-toggle" data-area="${a}" style="cursor:pointer;opacity:${off?0.4:1}">
          <div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${off?'#ccc':(AREA_COLORS[a]||'#888')}"></div>
          <span class="net-leg-name">${AREA_LABELS[a]||a}</span>
          <span class="net-leg-count">${cnt}</span></div>`;
      }).join('');
    legendEl.querySelectorAll('.net-leg-toggle').forEach(el=>{
      el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const a=el.dataset.area;
        if(hiddenAreas.has(a)) hiddenAreas.delete(a); else hiddenAreas.add(a);
        renderLegend();
        applyFilters();
      });
    });
  }
  renderLegend();

  // D3 graph
  const container=document.getElementById('network-graph');
  const W=container.clientWidth||700, H=container.clientHeight||620;
  const svg=d3.select(container).append('svg')
    .attr('width','100%').attr('height','100%')
    .attr('viewBox',`0 0 ${W} ${H}`)
    .style('border-radius','8px');
  const tooltip=d3.select(container).append('div').attr('class','net-tooltip');
  const infoCard=d3.select(container).append('div').attr('class','net-info-card');
  const g=svg.append('g');
  svg.call(d3.zoom().scaleExtent([0.3,5]).on('zoom',e=>g.attr('transform',e.transform)));

  function escHtml(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));}
  function buildInfoCard(d){
    const areaLabel=AREA_LABELS[d.area]||d.area||'—';
    const color=d.isAnchor?'#E63946':(AREA_COLORS[d.area]||'#64748b');
    const mainName=d.name||'Organização sem nome';
    const subName=d.isAnchor?'Organização Âncora'
                            :((d.fantasia&&d.fantasia!==d.name)?d.fantasia:null);
    let body=`<h4>${escHtml(mainName)}</h4>`;
    if(subName) body+=`<div class="net-card-sub">${escHtml(subName)}</div>`;
    if(d.isAnchor){
      body+=`<div class="net-card-tags"><span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${color}">Âncora</span>`;
      if(d.focus) body+=d.focus.map(f=>`<span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${AREA_COLORS[f]||'#888'}">${escHtml(AREA_LABELS[f]||f)}</span>`).join('');
      body+=`</div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Região</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.region||'—')}</div></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Bairro</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.bairro||'—')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.related&&d.related.length) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Áreas afins</div><div class="val">${d.related.map(r=>escHtml(AREA_LABELS[r]||r)).join(', ')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.desc) body+=`<div class="net-card-desc">${escHtml(d.desc)}</div>`;
    }else{
      body+=`<div class="net-card-tags"><span class="net-card-tag" style="background:${color}">${escHtml(areaLabel)}</span></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Região</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.region||'—')}</div></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Bairro</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.bairro||'—')}</div></div>`;
      if(d.end) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Endereço</div><div class="val" style="font-size:10.5px">${escHtml(d.end)}</div></div>`;
      if(d.fund) body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Fundação</div><div class="val">${escHtml(d.fund)}</div></div>`;
      const afinidade=d.thematic>=3?'Alta afinidade temática':d.thematic>=1?'Afinidade temática média':'Afinidade geográfica';
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Vínculo</div><div class="val"><span style="display:inline-block;width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:#E63946;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle"></span>CEASA · score ${d.score}</div></div>`;
      body+=`<div class="net-card-row"><div class="lbl">Afinidade</div><div class="val">${afinidade}</div></div>`;
    }
    return `<div class="net-card-accent" style="background:${color}"></div>`+
           `<button class="net-card-close" type="button" aria-label="Fechar">×</button>`+
           `<div class="net-card-body">${body}</div>`;
  }
  function showInfoCard(d){
    infoCard.html(buildInfoCard(d)).style('display','block');
    const btn=infoCard.node().querySelector('.net-card-close');
    if(btn) btn.addEventListener('click',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();infoCard.style('display','none');});
  }
  infoCard.on('click',ev=>ev.stopPropagation());

  const sim=d3.forceSimulation(nodes)
    .force('link',d3.forceLink(edges).id(d=>d.id).distance(d=>d.strength==='peer'?80:d.value>=5?130:180).strength(d=>d.strength==='peer'?0.2:d.value>=5?0.4:0.12))
    .force('charge',d3.forceManyBody().strength(d=>d.isAnchor?-1200:-90))
    .force('center',d3.forceCenter(W/2,H/2))
    .force('collision',d3.forceCollide().radius(22).strength(0.9))
    .force('x',d3.forceX(W/2).strength(0.04))
    .force('y',d3.forceY(H/2).strength(0.04));

  const strokeColor={strong:'rgba(230,57,70,.5)',medium:'rgba(124,58,237,.3)',light:'rgba(100,100,100,.15)',peer:'rgba(100,100,100,.08)'};
  const strokeW={strong:1.2,medium:0.6,light:0.3,peer:0.15};

  const link=g.append('g').selectAll('line').data(edges).join('line')
    .attr('stroke',d=>strokeColor[d.strength]).attr('stroke-width',d=>strokeW[d.strength])
    .attr('stroke-dasharray',d=>d.strength==='peer'?'3,3':null);

  const nodeG=g.append('g').selectAll('g').data(nodes).join('g')
    .call(d3.drag().on('start',(e,d)=>{if(!e.active)sim.alphaTarget(0.3).restart();d.fx=d.x;d.fy=d.y})
      .on('drag',(e,d)=>{d.fx=e.x;d.fy=e.y}).on('end',(e,d)=>{if(!e.active)sim.alphaTarget(0);d.fx=null;d.fy=null}));

  const nodeR=d=>d.isAnchor?22:Math.max(5,Math.min(13,(d.score||1)*1.8));
  nodeG.append('circle')
    .attr('r',nodeR)
    .attr('fill',d=>d.isAnchor?'#E63946':(AREA_COLORS[d.area]||'#888'))
    .attr('stroke',d=>d.isAnchor?'#fff':'rgba(255,255,255,.7)')
    .attr('stroke-width',d=>d.isAnchor?3:1.5)
    .style('cursor','pointer')
    .style('filter',d=>d.isAnchor?'drop-shadow(0 2px 6px rgba(230,57,70,.5))':null);

  nodeG.filter(d=>d.isAnchor).append('text')
    .text('CEASA').attr('text-anchor','middle').attr('dy','.35em')
    .attr('fill','#fff').attr('font-size','9px').attr('font-weight','800').attr('letter-spacing','.05em')
    .style('pointer-events','none');

  // Dynamic labels (hidden by default, shown on filter)
  nodeG.filter(d=>!d.isAnchor).append('text').attr('class','net-label')
    .text(d=>d.shortName||d.name.slice(0,25))
    .attr('dx',d=>nodeR(d)+4).attr('dy','.35em')
    .attr('fill','var(--text)').attr('font-size','8px').attr('font-weight','600')
    .style('pointer-events','none').style('display','none');

  // Hover
  nodeG.on('mouseover',function(e,d){
    d3.select(this).select('circle').transition().duration(150).attr('r',d.isAnchor?26:nodeR(d)*1.5);
    link.transition().duration(150).attr('stroke-opacity',l=>(l.source===d||l.target===d)?1:0.1);
    nodeG.transition().duration(150).style('opacity',n=>{
      if(n===d) return 1;
      return edges.some(e=>(e.source===d&&e.target===n)||(e.target===d&&e.source===n))?1:0.15;
    });
    const areaLabel=AREA_LABELS[d.area]||d.area;
    const color=d.isAnchor?'#E63946':(AREA_COLORS[d.area]||'#888');
    tooltip.style('opacity',1).html(
      `<strong>${d.name}</strong>`+
      (d.bairro?`<br>${d.bairro}${d.region?' · '+d.region:''}`:'')+
      `<br><span class="net-tag" style="background:${color};color:#fff">${areaLabel}</span>`+
      (!d.isAnchor?`<br>Afinidade: ${d.thematic>=3?'Alta':d.thematic>=1?'Média':'Geográfica'} · Score: ${d.score}`:'')
    );
    const rect=container.getBoundingClientRect();
    tooltip.style('left',Math.min(e.pageX-rect.left+12,W-220)+'px').style('top',(e.pageY-rect.top-60)+'px');
  }).on('mouseout',function(e,d){
    d3.select(this).select('circle').transition().duration(200).attr('r',nodeR(d));
    link.transition().duration(200).attr('stroke-opacity',1);
    nodeG.transition().duration(200).style('opacity',1);
    tooltip.style('opacity',0);
  }).on('click',function(e,d){
    e.stopPropagation();
    showInfoCard(d);
  });

  // Clicking on empty graph area closes the card
  svg.on('click',()=>{infoCard.style('display','none');});

  sim.on('tick',()=>{
    link.attr('x1',d=>d.source.x).attr('y1',d=>d.source.y).attr('x2',d=>d.target.x).attr('y2',d=>d.target.y);
    nodeG.attr('transform',d=>`translate(${d.x},${d.y})`);
  });

  // Unified filter logic
  function applyFilters(){
    const bairroFilter=bairroSel.value;
    const regiaoFilter=regiaoSel.value;
    const hasFilter=activeDeg!=='all'||bairroFilter!==''||regiaoFilter!=='';

    // Determine visible nodes
    nodeG.each(function(d){
      if(d.isAnchor){d._visible=true;return;}
      let vis=true;
      if(hiddenAreas.has(d.area)) vis=false;
      if(regiaoFilter&&d.region!==regiaoFilter) vis=false;
      if(bairroFilter&&d.bairro!==bairroFilter) vis=false;
      d._visible=vis;
    });

    // Links visibility
    link.each(function(d){
      const src=d.source,tgt=d.target;
      let vis=src._visible!==false&&tgt._visible!==false;
      if(vis&&activeDeg!=='all'){
        vis=d.strength===activeDeg;
      }
      d._linkVis=vis;
      d3.select(this).attr('display',vis?null:'none');
    });

    // If degree filter active, hide nodes with no visible links
    if(activeDeg!=='all'){
      nodeG.each(function(d){
        if(d.isAnchor) return;
        if(!d._visible) return;
        const hasLink=edges.some(e=>e._linkVis&&(e.source===d||e.target===d));
        d._visible=hasLink;
      });
    }

    nodeG.attr('display',d=>d._visible?null:'none');

    // Smart labels: always show when degree filter active, or when ≤80 nodes with other filters
    let visCount=0;
    nodeG.each(d=>{if(d._visible&&!d.isAnchor)visCount++;});
    const showLabels=activeDeg!=='all'||(hasFilter&&visCount<=80);
    nodeG.selectAll('.net-label').style('display',d=>(showLabels&&d._visible)?null:'none');

    // Re-heat simulation to spread out filtered nodes
    sim.alpha(0.4).restart();
  }

  bairroSel.addEventListener('change',applyFilters);

  /* ── Fullscreen region/bairro filters (synced) ── */
  const fsRegiao=document.getElementById('net-fs-regiao');
  const fsBairroSel=document.getElementById('net-fs-bairro');
  regioes.forEach(r=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=r;o.textContent=r;fsRegiao.appendChild(o);});
  function syncFsBairros(region){
    fsBairroSel.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos os bairros</option>';
    const list=region?allBairros.filter(b=>(REGION_MAP[b]||'')===region):allBairros;
    list.forEach(b=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=b;o.textContent=b;fsBairroSel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  syncFsBairros('');
  // Sync FS filters → main filters
  fsRegiao.addEventListener('change',()=>{regiaoSel.value=fsRegiao.value;syncFsBairros(fsRegiao.value);fsBairroSel.value='';populateBairros(regiaoSel.value);bairroSel.value='';applyFilters();});
  fsBairroSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{bairroSel.value=fsBairroSel.value;applyFilters();});
  // Sync main filters → FS filters
  regiaoSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{fsRegiao.value=regiaoSel.value;syncFsBairros(fsRegiao.value);fsBairroSel.value='';});
  bairroSel.addEventListener('change',()=>{fsBairroSel.value=bairroSel.value;});

  /* ── Fullscreen legend (mirror of main) ── */
  const fsLegendEl=document.getElementById('net-fs-legend');
  function renderFsLegend(){
    const sorted=legendAreas.slice().sort((a,b)=>(areaCount[b]||0)-(areaCount[a]||0));
    fsLegendEl.innerHTML=
      `<div class="net-leg"><div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:#E63946"></div><span class="net-leg-name" style="font-weight:600">CEASA (âncora)</span></div>`+
      sorted.map(a=>{
        const off=hiddenAreas.has(a);const cnt=areaCount[a]||0;
        return `<div class="net-leg" data-area="${a}" style="cursor:pointer;opacity:${off?0.4:1}">
          <div class="net-leg-dot" style="background:${off?'#ccc':(AREA_COLORS[a]||'#888')}"></div>
          <span class="net-leg-name">${AREA_LABELS[a]||a}</span>
          <span class="net-leg-count">${cnt}</span></div>`;
      }).join('');
    fsLegendEl.querySelectorAll('.net-leg[data-area]').forEach(el=>{
      el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        const a=el.dataset.area;
        if(hiddenAreas.has(a)) hiddenAreas.delete(a); else hiddenAreas.add(a);
        renderLegend();renderFsLegend();applyFilters();
      });
    });
  }
  // Patch main renderLegend to also update FS legend
  const _origRenderLegend=renderLegend;
  renderLegend=function(){_origRenderLegend();renderFsLegend();};
  renderFsLegend();

  /* ── Fullscreen toggle ── */
  const expandBtn=document.getElementById('net-expand-btn');
  const backdrop=document.getElementById('net-fs-backdrop');
  let isFullscreen=false;
  function toggleFullscreen(){
    isFullscreen=!isFullscreen;
    container.classList.toggle('net-fullscreen',isFullscreen);
    backdrop.style.display=isFullscreen?'block':'none';
    expandBtn.textContent=isFullscreen?'✕':'⛶';
    expandBtn.title=isFullscreen?'Fechar tela cheia':'Expandir visualização';
    document.body.style.overflow=isFullscreen?'hidden':'';
    // Sync FS filter state
    fsRegiao.value=regiaoSel.value;
    syncFsBairros(fsRegiao.value);
    fsBairroSel.value=bairroSel.value;
    // Resize SVG viewBox
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const nW=container.clientWidth,nH=container.clientHeight;
      svg.attr('viewBox',`0 0 ${nW} ${nH}`);
      sim.force('center',d3.forceCenter(nW/2,nH/2));
      sim.force('x',d3.forceX(nW/2).strength(0.03));
      sim.force('y',d3.forceY(nH/2).strength(0.03));
      sim.alpha(0.3).restart();
    },100);
  }
  expandBtn.addEventListener('click',toggleFullscreen);
  backdrop.addEventListener('click',toggleFullscreen);
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&isFullscreen)toggleFullscreen();});
}

const AREA_COLORS = {
  'Religiao':'#8e44ad',

const AREA_LABELS = {

  /* Extremo Norte — bairros mais setentrionais, caráter rural/periurbano */
